<!-- Tab: About -->

<section id="school-panel-about" class="school-panel hidden max-w-4xl mx-auto px-4 py-12 space-y-6">

<h2 class="text-2xl font-serif-jp font-bold text-slate-900 border-b border-slate-200 pb-3">学園案内・教育理念</h2>

<p class="text-slate-700 leading-relaxed">

1958年の創立以来、私立星陵学園は「誠実・探究・共生」を校訓とし、変化の激しい社会において自ら考え行動できる人材の育成を目指してきました。

</p>

<div class="grid sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4">

<div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100">

<h3 class="font-bold text-sky-900 mb-1">建学の精神</h3>

<p class="text-sm text-slate-700">真理を愛し、他者を尊重し、社会に貢献する知性と品格を養う。</p>

</div>

<div class="p-4 rounded-xl bg-sky-50 border border-sky-100">

<h3 class="font-bold text-sky-900 mb-1">生徒数・教員数</h3>

<p class="text-sm text-slate-700">中学・高校あわせて約1,080名の生徒が在籍しています。</p>

</div>

</div>

</section>

function decodeB64Utf8(str) {

const bin = atob(str);

const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length);

for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);

return new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);

}

title: 'NEON ARCADE',

code: decodeB64Utf8(PRESET_NEON_B64)

};

function loadState() {

try {

['eclipse_portal_state_v1', 'eclipse_portal_state_v2', 'eclipse_portal_state_v3', 'eclipse_portal_state_v4'].forEach((k) => localStorage.removeItem(k));

} catch (e) {}

return {

coverTheme: defaultState.coverTheme,

passcode: defaultState.passcode,

savedCodes: [Object.assign({}, PRESET_NEON_ITEM)],

activeCodeId: PRESET_NEON_ITEM.id,

missions: defaultState.missions.map((m) => Object.assign({}, m)),

files: defaultState.files.map((f) => Object.assign({}, f))

};

}


function saveState() {

// 再読み込みで初期状態（NEON ARCADEのみ搭載・パスコード7373）にリセットされるため永続保存しない

}


const state = loadState();

let inputCode = '';

let logoClickCount = 0;

let logoClickTimer = null;

let editingCodeId = null;


let audioCtx = null;

function playBeep(freq, duration) {

try {

if (!audioCtx) {

const Ctx = window.AudioContext || window.webkitAudioContext;

if (!Ctx) return;

audioCtx = new Ctx();

}

const osc = audioCtx.createOscillator();

const gain = audioCtx.createGain();

osc.type = 'sine';

osc.frequency.value = freq;

gain.gain.value = 0.04;

osc.connect(gain);

gain.connect(audioCtx.destination);

osc.start();

osc.stop(audioCtx.currentTime + duration);

} catch (e) {}

}

const keypadBox = document.getElementById('keypad-box');

const keypadDisplay = document.getElementById('keypad-display');

const keypadStatus = document.getElementById('keypad-status');

const decryptOverlay = document.getElementById('decrypt-overlay');

const decryptProgress = document.getElementById('decrypt-progress');

const secretView = document.getElementById('secret-view');

const deepSecretView = document.getElementById('deep-secret-view');

const deepEditorPanel = document.getElementById('deep-editor-panel');

const deepEditorHeading = document.getElementById('deep-editor-heading');

const deepTitleInput = document.getElementById('deep-title-input');

const deepCodeInput = document.getElementById('deep-code-input');

const deepCodeFrame = document.getElementById('deep-code-frame');

const deepSavedList = document.getElementById('deep-saved-list');

const deepFullscreenOverlay = document.getElementById('deep-fullscreen-overlay');

const deepFsCloseBtn = document.getElementById('deep-fs-close-btn');

const toastBox = document.getElementById('toast-box');


function showToast(msg) {

toastBox.textContent = msg;

toastBox.classList.remove('hidden');

clearTimeout(showToast._t);

showToast._t = setTimeout(() => toastBox.classList.add('hidden'), 2600);

}


function applyCoverTheme() {

if (state.coverTheme === 'news') {

themeSchool.classList.add('hidden');

themeNews.classList.remove('hidden');

document.title = 'METRO DAILY NEWS | 最新ニュース・速報';

} else {

themeNews.classList.add('hidden');

themeSchool.classList.remove('hidden');

document.title = '私立 星陵学園 公式ホームページ | Seiryo Academy';

}

const label = document.getElementById('status-cover-label');

if (label) {

label.textContent = state.coverTheme === 'news' ? 'ニュースサイト偽装中' : '学校サイト偽装中';

}

const savedCountEl = document.getElementById('status-saved-count');

if (savedCountEl) {

savedCountEl.textContent = state.savedCodes.length + ' 件';

}

document.querySelectorAll('.cover-choice-btn').forEach((btn) => {

const active = btn.getAttribute('data-set-cover') === state.coverTheme;

btn.classList.toggle('border-emerald-500/60', active);

btn.classList.toggle('bg-emerald-500/10', active);

btn.classList.toggle('border-slate-700', !active);

btn.classList.toggle('bg-slate-950/60', !active);

});

}


function switchSchoolTab(tabName) {

document.querySelectorAll('.school-panel').forEach((p) => p.classList.add('hidden'));

const target = document.getElementById('school-panel-' + tabName);

if (target) target.classList.remove('hidden');


document.querySelectorAll('.school-nav-btn').forEach((b) => {

const active = b.getAttribute('data-school-tab') === tabName;

b.classList.toggle('text-sky-800', active);

b.classList.toggle('bg-sky-50', active);

b.classList.toggle('text-slate-600', !active);

});

document.getElementById('school-mobile-nav').classList.add('hidden');

}


document.querySelectorAll('[data-school-tab]').forEach((btn) => {

btn.addEventListener('click', () => switchSchoolTab(btn.getAttribute('data-school-tab')));

});


document.getElementById('school-mobile-menu-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {

document.getElementById('school-mobile-nav').classList.toggle('hidden');

});


document.querySelectorAll('.news-cat-btn').forEach((btn) => {

btn.addEventListener('click', () => {

const cat = btn.getAttribute('data-news-cat');

document.querySelectorAll('.news-cat-btn').forEach((b) => b.classList.remove('text-rose-600'));

btn.classList.add('text-rose-600');

document.querySelectorAll('.news-card').forEach((card) => {

const match = cat === 'all' || card.getAttribute('data-cat') === cat;

card.classList.toggle('hidden', !match);

});

});

});


function openKeypad() {

inputCode = '';

updateKeypadDisplay();

keypadStatus.textContent = 'Enter Access Code';

keypadStatus.className = 'font-mono-tech text-[11px] tracking-wider text-slate-400 uppercase';

keypadModal.classList.remove('hidden');

playBeep(660, 0.08);

}


function closeKeypad() {

keypadModal.classList.add('hidden');

inputCode = '';

}


function handleLogoTrigger() {

logoClickCount += 1;

clearTimeout(logoClickTimer);

if (logoClickCount === 4) {

playBeep(520, 0.04);

}

if (logoClickCount >= 5) {

logoClickCount = 0;

openKeypad();

return;

}

logoClickTimer = setTimeout(() => {

logoClickCount = 0;

}, 2000);

}


document.getElementById('school-logo-trigger').addEventListener('click', handleLogoTrigger);

document.getElementById('news-logo-trigger').addEventListener('click', handleLogoTrigger);

document.getElementById('school-year-trigger').addEventListener('dblclick', openKeypad);

document.getElementById('news-year-trigger').addEventListener('dblclick', openKeypad);

document.getElementById('keypad-close-btn').addEventListener('click', closeKeypad);


function updateKeypadDisplay() {

const len = Math.max(state.passcode.length, 4);

const masked = '●'.repeat(inputCode.length);

const dashes = '-'.repeat(Math.max(0, len - inputCode.length));

keypadDisplay.textContent = masked + dashes;

}


function appendDigit(digit) {

if (inputCode.length >= 8) return;

inputCode += digit;

playBeep(880, 0.04);

updateKeypadDisplay();

}


function submitPasscode() {

if (inputCode === state.passcode) {

playBeep(1046, 0.12);

keypadStatus.textContent = 'ACCESS GRANTED';

keypadStatus.className = 'font-mono-tech text-[11px] tracking-wider text-emerald-400 uppercase font-bold';

setTimeout(() => {

closeKeypad();

enterSecretSite();

}, 260);

} else {

playBeep(220, 0.18);

keypadStatus.textContent = 'ACCESS DENIED';

keypadStatus.className = 'font-mono-tech text-[11px] tracking-wider text-rose-400 uppercase font-bold';

keypadBox.className = keypadBox.className;

keypadBox.classList.remove('animate-shake');

void keypadBox.offsetWidth;

keypadBox.classList.add('animate-shake');

inputCode = '';

updateKeypadDisplay();

}

}


document.querySelectorAll('.kp-btn').forEach((btn) => {

btn.addEventListener('click', () => appendDigit(btn.getAttribute('data-key')));

});


document.getElementById('kp-clear').addEventListener('click', () => {

inputCode = '';

playBeep(440, 0.04);

updateKeypadDisplay();

});


document.getElementById('kp-enter').addEventListener('click', submitPasscode);


function enterSecretSite() {

decryptOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');

decryptProgress.style.width = '0%';

setTimeout(() => { decryptProgress.style.width = '100%'; }, 40);

setTimeout(() => {

decryptOverlay.classList.add('hidden');

surfaceView.classList.add('hidden');

deepSecretView.classList.add('hidden');

secretView.classList.remove('hidden');

applyCoverTheme();

renderMissions();

renderClassifiedFiles();

lucide.createIcons();

}, 780);

}


// ===== Multi-HTML Code Bank Logic =====

function launchFullscreenCode(item) {

state.activeCodeId = item.id;

deepCodeFrame.srcdoc = item.code || defaultDeepCode;

deepFullscreenOverlay.classList.remove('hidden');

}


function closeFullscreenCode() {

deepFullscreenOverlay.classList.add('hidden');

deepCodeFrame.srcdoc = 'about:blank';

}


deepFsCloseBtn.addEventListener('click', closeFullscreenCode);


function renderDeepBank() {

deepSavedList.innerHTML = '';

if (state.savedCodes.length === 0) {

const empty = document.createElement('div');

empty.className = 'p-6 rounded-xl border border-slate-800 bg-slate-900/60 text-center text-xs font-mono-tech text-slate-400';

empty.textContent = '保存されているHTMLはありません。「＋ 新しいHTMLを追加」から登録できます。';

deepSavedList.appendChild(empty);

return;

}


state.savedCodes.forEach((item, idx) => {

const row = document.createElement('div');

row.className = 'flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-4 rounded-xl bg-slate-900/85 border border-slate-800 hover:border-cyan-500/40 transition';


const info = document.createElement('div');

info.className = 'min-w-0 space-y-1';

const titleEl = document.createElement('div');

titleEl.className = 'flex items-center gap-2';

const badge = document.createElement('span');

badge.className = 'px-2 py-0.5 rounded bg-cyan-500/15 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-500/30 text-[10px] font-mono-tech shrink-0';

badge.textContent = '#' + String(idx + 1).padStart(2, '0');

const name = document.createElement('span');

name.className = 'font-bold text-sm sm:text-base text-white truncate';

name.textContent = item.title;

titleEl.append(badge, name);


const meta = document.createElement('p');

meta.className = 'text-xs font-mono-tech text-slate-400';

meta.textContent = 'サイズ: ' + item.code.length.toLocaleString() + ' 文字';

info.append(titleEl, meta);


const actions = document.createElement('div');

actions.className = 'flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 shrink-0';


const launchBtn = document.createElement('button');

launchBtn.type = 'button';

launchBtn.className = 'px-4 py-2 rounded-lg bg-cyan-500 hover:bg-cyan-400 text-slate-950 font-mono-tech font-bold text-xs tracking-wider transition';

launchBtn.textContent = '全画面で開く ↗';

launchBtn.addEventListener('click', () => launchFullscreenCode(item));


const editBtn = document.createElement('button');

editBtn.type = 'button';

editBtn.className = 'px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-200 font-mono-tech text-xs transition';

editBtn.textContent = '編集';

editBtn.addEventListener('click', () => openDeepEditor(item));


const delBtn = document.createElement('button');

delBtn.type = 'button';

delBtn.className = 'px-3 py-2 rounded-lg bg-slate-800 hover:bg-rose-900/60 text-rose-400 font-mono-tech text-xs transition';

delBtn.textContent = '削除';

delBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {

state.savedCodes.splice(idx, 1);

if (state.activeCodeId === item.id) {

state.activeCodeId = state.savedCodes.length > 0 ? state.savedCodes[0].id : null;

}

saveState();

renderDeepBank();

applyCoverTheme();

showToast('「' + item.title + '」を削除しました');

});


actions.append(launchBtn, editBtn, delBtn);

row.append(info, actions);

deepSavedList.appendChild(row);

});

}


function openDeepEditor(item) {

if (item) {

editingCodeId = item.id;

deepEditorHeading.textContent = '// コードの編集: ' + item.title;

deepTitleInput.value = item.title;

deepCodeInput.value = item.code;

} else {

editingCodeId = null;

deepEditorHeading.textContent = '// 新しいHTMLコードを追加';

deepTitleInput.value = '';

deepCodeInput.value = '';

}

deepEditorPanel.classList.remove('hidden');

}


function openDeepCore() {

secretView.classList.add('hidden');

deepSecretView.classList.remove('hidden');

renderDeepBank();

deepEditorPanel.classList.add('hidden');

lucide.createIcons();

}


document.getElementById('enter-deep-core-btn').addEventListener('click', openDeepCore);

document.getElementById('enter-deep-core-card-btn').addEventListener('click', openDeepCore);


document.getElementById('back-to-layer1-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {

closeFullscreenCode();

deepSecretView.classList.add('hidden');

secretView.classList.remove('hidden');

applyCoverTheme();

});


document.getElementById('deep-new-code-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {

openDeepEditor(null);

});


document.getElementById('deep-cancel-edit-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {

deepEditorPanel.classList.add('hidden');

});


document.getElementById('deep-run-code-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {

const code = deepCodeInput.value;

if (!code.trim()) {

showToast('HTMLコードを入力してください');

return;

}

const title = deepTitleInput.value.trim() || ('秘密ページ ' + (state.savedCodes.length + 1));

let targetItem = null;


if (editingCodeId) {

targetItem = state.savedCodes.find((c) => c.id === editingCodeId);

if (targetItem) {

targetItem.title = title;

targetItem.code = code;

}

} else {

const newId = 'code_' + Date.now();

targetItem = { id: newId, title, code };

state.savedCodes.push(targetItem);

}


saveState();

deepEditorPanel.classList.add('hidden');

renderDeepBank();

applyCoverTheme();

if (targetItem) launchFullscreenCode(targetItem);

});


function panicLock() {

closeFullscreenCode();

secretView.classList.add('hidden');

deepSecretView.classList.add('hidden');

closeKeypad();

surfaceView.classList.remove('hidden');

applyCoverTheme();

}


document.getElementById('panic-lock-btn').addEventListener('click', panicLock);

document.getElementById('deep-panic-btn').addEventListener('click', panicLock);


document.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {

if (e.key === 'Escape') {

if (!secretView.classList.contains('hidden') || !deepSecretView.classList.contains('hidden')) {

panicLock();

} else if (!keypadModal.classList.contains('hidden')) {

closeKeypad();

}

return;

}

if (e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'K' || e.key === 'k') && secretView.classList.contains('hidden') && deepSecretView.classList.contains('hidden')) {

openKeypad();

return;

}

if (!keypadModal.classList.contains('hidden')) {

if (/^\d$/.test(e.key)) {

appendDigit(e.key);

} else if (e.key === 'Backspace') {

inputCode = inputCode.slice(0, -1);

updateKeypadDisplay();

} else if (e.key === 'Enter') {

submitPasscode();

}

}

});


document.querySelectorAll('.secret-tab-btn').forEach((btn) => {

btn.addEventListener('click', () => {

const tab = btn.getAttribute('data-secret-tab');

document.querySelectorAll('.secret-panel').forEach((p) => p.classList.add('hidden'));

document.getElementById('secret-panel-' + tab).classList.remove('hidden');

document.querySelectorAll('.secret-tab-btn').forEach((b) => {

const active = b === btn;

b.classList.toggle('bg-emerald-500/20', active);

b.classList.toggle('text-emerald-300', active);

b.classList.toggle('border', active);

b.classList.toggle('border-emerald-500/40', active);

b.classList.toggle('text-slate-400', !active);

});

});

});


function renderMissions() {

const list = document.getElementById('mission-list');

list.innerHTML = '';

let activeCount = 0;

state.missions.forEach((m) => {

if (!m.done) activeCount++;

const li = document.createElement('li');

li.className = 'flex items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 rounded-lg bg-slate-950/70 border border-slate-800 hover:border-emerald-500/40 cursor-pointer transition';

li.innerHTML = `

<div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">

<span class="w-4 h-4 rounded border flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${m.done ? 'bg-emerald-500 border-emerald-500 text-slate-950' : 'border-slate-600'}">

${m.done ? '✓' : ''}

</span>

<span class="text-sm truncate ${m.done ? 'line-through text-slate-500' : 'text-slate-100'}"></span>

</div>

<span class="px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-mono-tech shrink-0 ${m.rank === 'TOP SECRET' ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300' : m.rank === 'CONFIDENTIAL' ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'}">${m.rank}</span>

`;

li.querySelector('span.text-sm').textContent = m.title;

li.addEventListener('click', () => {

m.done = !m.done;

saveState();

renderMissions();

});

list.appendChild(li);

});

document.getElementById('status-mission-count').textContent = activeCount + ' 件';

}


document.getElementById('mission-form').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {

e.preventDefault();

const input = document.getElementById('mission-input');

const rank = document.getElementById('mission-rank').value;

const val = input.value.trim();

if (!val) return;

state.missions.unshift({ id: Date.now(), title: val, rank, done: false });

input.value = '';

saveState();

renderMissions();

showToast('新規指令を登録しました');

});


function renderClassifiedFiles() {

const grid = document.getElementById('classified-grid');

grid.innerHTML = '';

state.files.forEach((f) => {

const card = document.createElement('div');

card.className = 'bg-slate-900/80 border border-slate-800 hover:border-emerald-500/40 rounded-xl p-5 space-y-3 cursor-pointer transition';

card.innerHTML = `

<div class="flex items-center justify-between">

<span class="font-mono-tech text-xs text-emerald-400">${f.id}</span>

<span class="text-[10px] font-mono-tech px-2 py-0.5 rounded ${f.decrypted ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300' : 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-400'}">

${f.decrypted ? 'DECRYPTED' : 'LOCKED'}

</span>

</div>

<h3 class="font-bold text-white text-sm">${f.title}</h3>

<p class="text-xs leading-relaxed font-mono-tech ${f.decrypted ? 'text-slate-200' : 'text-slate-500'}">${f.decrypted ? f.plain : f.cipher}</p>

`;

card.addEventListener('click', () => {

f.decrypted = !f.decrypted;

playBeep(f.decrypted ? 920 : 520, 0.05);

renderClassifiedFiles();

});

grid.appendChild(card);

});

}


document.querySelectorAll('[data-set-cover]').forEach((btn) => {

btn.addEventListener('click', () => {

state.coverTheme = btn.getAttribute('data-set-cover');

saveState();

applyCoverTheme();

showToast('表サイトの偽装テーマを更新しました');

});

});


document.getElementById('passcode-form').addEventListener('submit', (e) => {

e.preventDefault();

const input = document.getElementById('new-passcode-input');

const feedback = document.getElementById('passcode-feedback');

const val = input.value.trim();

if (!/^\d{4,8}$/.test(val)) {

feedback.textContent = 'エラー：4〜8桁の半角数字で入力してください。';

feedback.className = 'text-xs font-mono-tech text-rose-400';

return;

}

state.passcode = val;

saveState();

input.value = '';

feedback.textContent = 'パスコードを更新しました（再読み込みすると7373にリセットされます）';

feedback.className = 'text-xs font-mono-tech text-emerald-400';

showToast('パスコードを変更しました');

});


setInterval(() => {

const el = document.getElementById('secret-clock');

if (el) {

const now = new Date();

el.textContent = now.toTimeString().slice(0, 8);

}

}, 1000);


applyCoverTheme();

lucide.createIcons();

})();

</script>

</body>
